=== PROMPT ===
You will be given a competitive programming problem.
There are N people divided into three teams.
The people are numbered 1, 2, \ldots, N, and the teams are numbered 1, 2, 3. Currently, person i belongs to team A_i.
Each person has a value called strength; person i has a strength of B_i. The strength of a team is defined as the sum of the strengths of its members.
Determine whether it is possible for zero or more people to switch teams so that all teams have equal strength. If it is possible, find the minimum number of people who need to switch teams to achieve this.
You cannot create new teams other than teams 1, 2, 3.

Input

The input is given from Standard Input in the following format:
N
A_1 B_1
A_2 B_2
\vdots
A_N B_N

Output

If it is possible to make all teams have equal strength, print the minimum number of people who need to switch teams. Otherwise, print -1.

Constraints


- 3 \leq N \leq 100
- A_i \in \lbrace 1, 2, 3 \rbrace
- For each x \in \lbrace 1, 2, 3 \rbrace, there exists some i with A_i = x.
- 1 \leq B_i
- \displaystyle\sum_{i = 1}^{N} B_i \leq 1500 
- All input values are integers.

Sample Input 1

6
1 2
2 5
1 5
3 3
1 3
3 6

Sample Output 1

2

If person 1 switches to team 3 and person 4 switches to team 2, all teams will have a strength of 8.

Sample Input 2

4
1 1
1 2
2 3
3 4

Sample Output 2

-1

Sample Input 3

3
1 1
2 1
3 1

Sample Output 3

0

Sample Input 4

12
2 5
1 4
3 3
2 3
3 9
1 2
2 2
3 9
2 6
1 9
1 1
3 1

Sample Output 4

3

Reason in a commit-implement-recheck way.

A productive rhythm for this task usually looks like this:
- Isolate the decisive constraints, the target complexity, and at most one or two approaches that are still truly plausible.
- If two candidates remain, compare them only on the decisive constraint and commit quickly to the stronger one instead of keeping a wide search open.
- Before coding, keep only a tiny implementation ledger for the chosen plan: main invariant, key state or transition, and the one or two edge cases most likely to fail.
- Move into code as soon as that ledger is clear. After coding starts, keep reasoning attached to the current draft rather than reopening the high-level search.
- After the first complete draft, do one short implementation-check-adjust-check loop on the same draft: test the key edge case, patch locally if needed, and recheck.
- Reopen the main design only if a concrete counterexample breaks the core invariant. Otherwise prefer the local patch and keep closing.
- Once the current draft, invariant, and short recheck still agree, finalize the solution.

Try to avoid patterns like these:
- spending too long comparing nearby plans before committing to one workable approach;
- returning from code to broad algorithm discussion without a concrete contradiction;
- turning one local doubt into a full restart when the current draft only needs a small patch.

Output only the final Python code enclosed in a code block.

Implementation with 2D list and valid range:

dp = [[INF]*(T+1) for _ in range(T+1)]
dp[0][0]=0
P=0
for a,b in people:
    ndp = [[INF]*(T+1) for _ in range(T+1)]
    # loop s1 from 0 to min(T, P) maybe also s1<=T
    max_s1 = min(T, P)
    for s1 in range(max_s1+1):
        row = dp[s1]
        # s2 range:
        # s2 <= T
        # s1+s2 <= P => s2 <= P-s1
        # s3 = P-s1-s2 <= T => s2 >= P-s1-T
        # s2 >=0
        low = max(0, P - s1 - T)
        high = min(T, P - s1)
        if low > high: continue
        # maybe row slice? We need values.
        for s2 in range(low, high+1):
            val = row[s2]
            if val == INF: continue
            # transition 3: assign to team a? Wait cost depends on a.
            # assign to team1
            if s1 + b <= T:
                nv = val + (0 if a==1 else 1)
                if nv < ndp[s1+b][s2]: ndp[s1+b][s2] = nv
            # assign to team2
            if s2 + b <= T:
                nv = val + (0 if a==2 else 1)
                if nv < ndp[s1][s2+b]: ndp[s1][s2+b] = nv
            # assign to team3
            if s3 + b <= T: # s3 = P - s1 - s2
                nv = val + (0 if a==3 else 1)
                if nv < ndp[s1][s2]: ndp[s1][s2] = nv
    dp = ndp
    P += b

Potential optimized implementation:

```
import sys

def solve():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return
    N = data[0]
    A = []
    B = []
    idx = 1
    total = 0
    maxb = 0
    for _ in range(N):
        a = data[idx]; b = data[idx+1]; idx += 2
        A.append(a); B.append(b)
        total += b
        if b > maxb: maxb = b
    if total % 3 != 0:
        print(-1); return
    T = total // 3
    if maxb > T:
        print(-1); return
    INF = 10**9
    W = T + 1
    dp = [[INF] * W for _ in range(W)]
    dp[0][0] = 0
    P = 0
    for a, b in zip(A, B):
        ndp = [[INF] * W for _ in range(W)]
        cost1 = 0 if a == 1 else 1
        cost2 = 0 if a == 2 else 1
        cost3 = 0 if a == 3 else 1
        # max_s1 = min(T, P)
        # But also s1 can be up to T; P maybe >T. Use range(min(T,P)+1)
        max_s1 = T if T < P else P
        for s1 in range(max_s1 + 1):
            row = dp[s1]
            low = P - s1 - T
            if low < 0:
                low = 0
            high = P - s1
            if high > T:
                high = T
            if low > high:
                continue
            # optional: if all row slice INF? no.
            for s2 in range(low, high + 1):
                val = row[s2]
                if val == INF:
                    continue
                # team1
                ns1 = s1 + b
                if ns1 <= T:
                    nv = val + cost1
                    nr = ndp[ns1]
                    if nv < nr[s2]:
                        nr[s2] = nv
                # team2
                ns2 = s2 + b
                if ns2 <= T:
                    nv = val + cost2
                    nr = ndp[s1]
                    if nv < nr[ns2]:
                        nr[ns2] = nv
                # team3
                s3 = P - s1 - s2
                if s3 + b <= T:
                    nv = val + cost3
                    if nv < ndp[s1][s2]:
                        ndp[s1][s2] = nv
        dp = ndp
        P += b
    ans = dp[T][T]
    print(ans if ans < INF else -1)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    solve()
```

=== ANSWER ===
```python
import sys

def solve():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data:
        return

    N = data[0]
    A = []
    B = []
    idx = 1
    total = 0
    max_b = 0

    for _ in range(N):
        a = data[idx]
        b = data[idx + 1]
        idx += 2
        A.append(a)
        B.append(b)
        total += b
        if b > max_b:
            max_b = b

    if total % 3 != 0:
        print(-1)
        return

    T = total // 3

    # A person with strength > T can never belong to any team of strength T.
    if max_b > T:
        print(-1)
        return

    INF = 10**9
    W = T + 1

    # dp[s1][s2] = minimum switches after processing some people,
    # where team 1 has sum s1 and team 2 has sum s2.
    dp = [[INF] * W for _ in range(W)]
    dp[0][0] = 0

    processed_sum = 0

    for a, b in zip(A, B):
        ndp = [[INF] * W for _ in range(W)]

        cost1 = 0 if a == 1 else 1
        cost2 = 0 if a == 2 else 1
        cost3 = 0 if a == 3 else 1

        max_s1 = min(T, processed_sum)

        for s1 in range(max_s1 + 1):
            row = dp[s1]

            # team3 sum = processed_sum - s1 - s2 must be in [0, T]
            low = processed_sum - s1 - T
            if low < 0:
                low = 0
            high = processed_sum - s1
            if high > T:
                high = T

            if low > high:
                continue

            ndp_s1 = ndp[s1]

            for s2 in range(low, high + 1):
                val = row[s2]
                if val == INF:
                    continue

                # Put this person into team 1
                ns1 = s1 + b
                if ns1 <= T:
                    nv = val + cost1
                    if nv < ndp[ns1][s2]:
                        ndp[ns1][s2] = nv

                # Put this person into team 2
                ns2 = s2 + b
                if ns2 <= T:
                    nv = val + cost2
                    if nv < ndp_s1[ns2]:
                        ndp_s1[ns2] = nv

                # Put this person into team 3
                s3 = processed_sum - s1 - s2
                if s3 + b <= T:
                    nv = val + cost3
                    if nv < ndp_s1[s2]:
                        ndp_s1[s2] = nv

        dp = ndp
        processed_sum += b

    ans = dp[T][T]
    print(ans if ans < INF else -1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    solve()
```
